    ABOUT CGN MINING                                                        關於中廣核礦業
    The Company is incorporated in the Cayman Islands and listed on the     本公司註冊地為開曼群島，為一家在聯交
    main board of the Stock Exchange (stock code: 01164). The Group         所主板上市的公司（股份代號：01164）。
    is primarily engaged in the investment and trading of natural uranium   本集團主要從事天然鈾資源的投資與貿
    resources, with a view to being one of the top suppliers of natural     易，旨在成為核電站天然鈾資源的主要供
    uranium resources for nuclear power plants.                             貨商之一。

all objectives and tasks with high quality. In terms of new project        方面，於2021年收購奧公司49%股權，助
development, the acquisition of the 49% equity interest in Ortalyk in      力公司鎖定更多天然鈾資源；在項目運營
2021, helping the Company secure more natural uranium resources.           方面，謝公司和奧公司圓滿完成生產計劃
In terms of project operations, Semizbay-U and Ortalyk successfully        和各項經濟指標；在天然鈾貿易方面，中
fulfilled production plans and various economic indicators. In terms of    廣核國際銷售公司牢牢把握天然鈾市場復
natural uranium trading, CGN Global firmly grasped the opportunity         甦的機會，助力公司營業額大幅增長；在
presented by the recovery of the natural uranium market, contributing      社會責任方面，公司設立環境、社會及管
to a significant increase in the Company’s revenue. In terms of social     治委員會，並獨立發佈環境、社會及管治
responsibility, the Company established an Environmental, Social           報告，展現綠色、低碳、負責任的公司形
and Governance (ESG) Committee and independently published an              象；在權益融資方面，公司以0.8港元╱
ESG Report, demonstrating a green, low-carbon and responsible              股價格增發10億股普通股實現募資8億港

    Chairman’s Statement
    主席致辭

    Looking back at 2025, the global economy moved forward amidst               回首2025年，全球經濟在波動中前行，全
    fluctuations, and the global energy landscape was reshaped through          球能源版圖在變革中重塑，核電作為基荷
    transformation. The strategic value of nuclear power as a baseload          能源的戰略價值被重新定義，天然鈾行業
    energy source has been redefined. The natural uranium industry has          也由「復甦期」正式邁入「價值重估與供需
    officially transitioned from a “recovery period” to a new phase of “value   再平衡」的新階段，推動了鈾資源需求增
    reassessment and supply-demand rebalancing”, driving growth in              長，為公司經營帶來機遇與挑戰。與此同
    demand for uranium resources and presenting both opportunities and          時，受全球地緣政治和供應鏈安全影響，
    challenges for the Company’s operations. At the same time, affected         多個國家加大對全球鈾資源的爭奪，哈薩
    by global geopolitics and supply chain security, many countries have        克斯坦稅收政策不斷收緊，資源開發難度
    intensified their competition for global uranium resources. Tax policies    加大，公司面臨的不確定性風險和成本壓
    in Kazakhstan have been continuously tightened, making resource             力增大。2025年，公司在複雜市場環境中
    development more difficult. The Company faces increased uncertainty         保持戰略定力，在波動市場中保持經營韌
    risks and cost pressures. In 2025, the Company maintained strategic         性，生產運營總體穩定，資源開發與貿易
    focus amidst a complex market environment, sustained operational            業務有序推進，實現年度溢利453百萬港
    resilience in a volatile market, and kept production and operations         元，較上年度增加32.4%。
    generally stable. Resource development and trading businesses
    progressed in an orderly manner, achieving a profit for the year of
    HK$453 million, an increase of 32.4% compared to the previous year.


    Looking ahead, the Company will continue to focus on its                    展望未來，公司將繼續聚焦海外天然鈾
    overseas natural uranium business, benchmark against high-                  業務，對標高質量發展要求，持續提升
    quality development requirements, continuously enhance its core             核心競爭力，在全球天然鈾市場中鞏固
    competitiveness, and consolidate its advantageous position in the           優勢地位。2026年，公司將固本強基、
    global natural uranium market. In 2026, the Company will consolidate        開拓奮進，總體工作思路為以「拓資源、
    its foundation and strive for progress. The overall work strategy is        穩經營、提價值、強支撐」為主線，錨定
    centered on “expanding resources, stabilizing operations, enhancing         新項目開發破局資源瓶頸，聚焦貿易風
    value, and strengthening support”, aiming to break through resource         險防控，生產經營穩健，實現可持續發
    bottlenecks through new project development, focus on trade risk            展。2026年，公司董事會將持續強化「定
    prevention, maintain stable production and operations, and achieve          戰略、作決策、防風險」核心功能，聚焦
    sustainable development. In 2026, the Board of the Company                  重大風險精準研判、風險管理體系優化完
    will continue to strengthen its core functions of “setting strategy,        善、重要領域重點防控三大方向，持續健
    making decisions, and preventing risks”, focusing on three major            全機制、強化數智支撐、嚴守安全底線，
    areas: accurate assessment of significant risks, optimization and           全面提升風險管理能力，為公司高質量發
    improvement of the risk management system, and key prevention               展提供堅實保障。
    and control in important areas. The Board will continuously improve
    mechanisms, strengthen digital and intelligent support, strictly
    adhere to safety bottom lines, and comprehensively enhance
    risk management capabilities to provide a solid guarantee for the
    Company’s high-quality development.

10   CGN Mining Company Limited   Annual Report 2025




     Chief Executive Officer’s Statement
     首席執行官致辭

     Dear Shareholders,                                                          各位股東，


     In 2025, the global nuclear power recovery continued to deepen, and         2025年，全球核能復甦持續深化，天然
     natural uranium prices steadily rebounded, while at the same time           鈾價格穩步回升，但同時面臨供給約束、
     facing multiple challenges including supply constraints, geopolitical       地緣政治擾動、成本上漲及稅收政策調整
     disruptions, rising costs, and tax policy adjustments. During the           等多重挑戰。本年度，公司雖上半年受鈾
     year, although the Company recorded a phased loss in the first half         價回落、貿易毛利下降影響出現階段性虧
     due to the decline in uranium prices and the decrease in trading            損，但堅守「資源開發+資本運營」雙輪驅
     gross profit, it adhered to the “resource development + capital             動戰略，聚焦經營核心，狠抓戰略落地、
     operation” dual-wheel drive strategy by focusing on core operations,        剛性推進計畫落實、持續深化管理提升、
     vigorously promoting strategy implementation, diligently advancing          全面強化風險管理，有效抵禦各類經營壓
     plan execution, further deepening management improvement,                   力，下半年實現經營回暖，全年順利完成
     comprehensively strengthening risk management, effectively resisting        核心經營目標。作為首席執行官，我謹代
     various operating pressures, which we achieved a business recovery          表公司管理層，向各位股東的信任支持、
     in the second half, and successfully completed core operating               全體員工的辛勤奉獻，致以誠摯感謝！
     targets for the full year. As the CEO, on behalf of the Company’s
     management, I would like to express my sincere gratitude to all
     Shareholders for your trust and support, and to all employees for their
     hard work and dedication!


     In 2025, the Company mainly focused on the following aspects of             2025年，公司主要致力於以下幾方面工

     Nuclear power market analysis                                             核電市場形勢分析

     First, regarding the advantages and necessity of nuclear power,           首先，在核電的優勢及必要性方面，核電
     nuclear power, as a baseload energy source with high energy               作為高能量密度、零碳排放且極具可靠性
     density, zero carbon emissions, and exceptional reliability, saw          的基荷能源，其戰略價值在2025年得到了
     its strategic value further elevated in 2025. With the explosive          進一步昇華。隨著全球人工智能及大規模
     growth of global artificial intelligence and large-scale data centers,    數據中心的爆發式增長，電力需求呈現剛
     electricity demand has experienced rigid expansion. Leveraging its        性擴張，核電憑借其極高的發電利用小時
     exceptionally high capacity factor and stable power output, nuclear       數和電力輸出穩定性，成為了科技巨頭實
     power has become the preferred energy source for technology giants        現「零碳算力」的首選能源；同時，在複雜
     seeking to achieve “zero-carbon computing power”. Simultaneously,         多變的能源安全環境下，核電在保障主權
     amid complex and evolving energy security landscapes, the role of         國家能源獨立、降低對波動性化石能源依
     nuclear power in safeguarding national energy independence and            賴方面的作用愈發凸顯。
     reducing dependence on volatile fossil fuels has become increasingly
     prominent.

between cost and safety. The Company aims to obtain competitive          顧的原則。公司致力追求具有競爭力的融

            and make recommendations as necessary from time to time;                 並於有需要時提出建議；

            development of Directors and senior management;                          訓及持續專業發展；

            ensure compliance with the legal and regulatory requirements;            法，以確保遵守法律及監管規定；

            or business ethics of the Company to ensure the Company’s                或經營道德標準的執行，以確保本

     CHAIRMAN AND CEO                                                          主席及首席執行官

     Under code provision C.2.1 of Part 2 of the Corporate Governance          根據企業管治守則第二部分守則條文第
     Code, the roles between the chairman and chief executive officer          C.2.1條，主席和首席執行官的角色應予
     should be separate and should not be performed by the same                以區分，且不應由一人同時兼任。由2025
     individual. From 1 January 2025 to 14 April 2025, Mr. Wang Xianfeng       年1月1日至2025年4月14日，王先鋒先生
     was both the chairman of the Board and the CEO. The Board believed        同時擔任董事會主席兼首席執行官。董事
     that Mr. Wang Xianfeng serving as both the chairman of the Board and      會認為，王先鋒先生同時擔任董事會主席
     the chief executive officer of the Company would ensure consistent        兼本公司首席執行官可確保領導及經營的
     leadership and operations. In order to make a clearer distinction         一致性。為更加清楚區分董事會主席及本
     between the responsibilities of the chairman of the Board and those of    公司首席執行官的職責，自2025年4月15
     the chief executive officer of the Company, the Board has appointed       日起董事會已聘任邱斌先生擔任首席執行
     Mr. Qiu Bin to serve as the CEO with effect from 15 April 2025.           官。

Internal control                                                            內部控制

The Board has overall responsibility for establishing and maintaining       董事會整體負責建立與維持公司內部控制
effective internal control system as well as reviewing its effectiveness,   體系的有效性，並不斷檢討其效力以保障
to safeguard the Group’s assets and Shareholders’ interest. The             本集團的資產及股東的利益。董事會每年
Board conducts annual reviews to ensure that the resources,                 進行檢討以確保公司在會計、內部審計、
staff qualifications and experience in relation to the Company’s            財務匯報職能方面以及與公司環境、社會
accounting, internal audit, and financial reporting; the Company’s          及管治表現和匯報相關的資源、員工資歷
environmental, social and governance performance and reporting;             及經驗，以及員工所接受的培訓課程及有
and the Company’s training courses and budgets thereof received             關預算是足夠的。公司各級管理層通過對
by staff are adequate. Management at all levels of the Company              內部監控系統的檢討來評估內部監控系統
evaluates the adequacy and effectiveness of the internal control            的充足性和有效性。內部控制監督部門每
system through regular reviews of the system. The internal control          年度向審核委員會匯報一次有關內部監控
regulatory department reports to the Audit Committee once a year            的主要調查結果，審核委員會向董事會匯
on key findings regarding internal controls. Audit Committee, in turn,      報有關內部監控系統中存在的重大事項和
communicates any material issues regarding internal control system          問題。
to the Board.

During the Reporting Period, the Audit Committee conducted a                報告期內，審核委員會已檢討內部控制
review on the effectiveness of the internal control system (including       系統的有效性（包括財務控制、運作控制
financial controls, operational controls and compliance controls), and      及合規控制），並無發現任何重大內控失
no significant internal control failings were identified. Therefore, the    誤。因此，董事會認為本集團的內部控制
Board considered that the internal control systems of the Group were        系統有效及足夠且符合企業管治守則的守
effective and sufficient, and in compliance with the code provisions of     則條文。
the Corporate Governance Code.

     Risk management and internal control                                     風險管理與內部控制

     Comprehensive risk management                                            全面風險管理

     The Company has in essence established a relatively comprehensive        本公司已基本建立起較為完善的全面風險
     risk management framework. With reference to the relevant risk           管理體系，以ISO31000:2018標準《風險
     management requirements of the “Risk Management Guidelines”              管理指南》及企業管治守則有關風險管理
     of ISO31000:2018 standard and the Corporate Governance Code,             的要求作為重要參考，對重大風險進行有
     the Company effectively identified, evaluated and managed the            效辨認、評估及管理，嚴格落實《全面風
     significant risks. It also strictly implemented the Comprehensive        險管理制度》，並將公司風險業務劃分為

      AUDITOR’S RESPONSIBILITIES FOR THE                                        核數師對審核綜合財務報表之
      AUDIT OF THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL                                       責任
      STATEMENTS
      Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether the       我們的目標乃對綜合財務報表整體是否不
      consolidated financial statements as a whole are free from material       存在由於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳
      misstatement, whether due to fraud or error, and to issue an auditor’s    述取得合理保證，並出具包括我們意見的
      report that includes our opinion. This report is made solely to you, as   核數師報告。本報告按照我們的委聘條款
      a body, in accordance with the terms of our engagement, and for no        僅向   閣下（作為一個整體）報告，除此之
      other purpose. We do not assume responsibility towards or accept          外，本報告不作其他用途。我們概不就本
      liability to any other person for the contents of this report.            報告的內容對任何其他人士承擔任何責任
                                                                                或負上任何法律責任。


      Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but is not a           合理保證為高水平的保證，但不能保證按
      guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with HKSAs will           照香港審核準則進行的審核在重大錯誤
      always detect a material misstatement when it exists. Misstatements       陳述存在時總能發現該錯誤陳述。錯誤陳
      can arise from fraud or error and are considered material if,             述可以由欺詐或錯誤引起，如果合理預期
      individually or in the aggregate, they could reasonably be expected to    其單獨或匯總起來可能影響綜合財務報表
      influence the economic decisions of users taken on the basis of these     使用者依賴綜合財務報表所作出的經濟決
      consolidated financial statements.                                        定，則被視為重大錯誤陳述。


      As part of an audit in accordance with HKSAs, we exercise                 根據香港審核準則進行審核時，我們運用
      professional judgement and maintain professional skepticism               專業判斷，於整個審核過程中保持專業懷
      throughout the audit. We also:                                            疑態度。我們亦：


      •      identify and assess the risks of material misstatement of the      •    識別及評估由於欺詐或錯誤而導致
             consolidated financial statements, whether due to fraud or              綜合財務報表存在重大錯誤陳述
             error, design and perform audit procedures responsive to                的風險，設計及執行審核程序以應
             those risks, and obtain audit evidence that is sufficient and           對該等風險，以及獲取充足及適當
             appropriate to provide a basis for our opinion. The risk of             的審核憑證，作為我們提供意見的
             not detecting a material misstatement resulting from fraud is           基礎。由於欺詐可能涉及串謀、偽
             higher than for one resulting from error, as fraud may involve          造、蓄意遺漏、虛假陳述，或凌駕
             collusion, forgery, intentional omissions, misrepresentations,          於內部控制之上，因此未能發現因
             or the override of internal control.                                    欺詐而導致出現重大錯誤陳述的風
                                                                                     險高於未能發現因錯誤而導致出現
                                                                                     重大錯誤陳述的風險。


      •      obtain an understanding of internal control relevant to the        •    了解與審核相關的內部控制，以設
             audit in order to design audit procedures that are appropriate          計在有關情況下屬適當的審核程
             in the circumstances, but not for the purpose of expressing an          序，但目的並非對   貴集團內部控
             opinion on the effectiveness of the Group’s internal control.           制的有效性發表意見。

           (iv)     Effective interest method                                           (iv)   實際利率法

                    The effective interest method is a method of                               實際利率法為計算
                    calculating the amortised cost of a financial                              金融資產或金融負
                    asset or financial liability and of allocating                             債的攤銷成本以及
                    interest income or interest expense over the                               於相關期間分配利
                    relevant period. The effective interest rate                               息收入或利息支出
                    is the rate that exactly discounts estimated                               的方法。實際利率
                    future cash receipts or payments through the                               乃為在金融資產或
                    expected life of the financial asset or liability, or                      負債的預計年期或
                    where appropriate, a shorter period.                                       （如 適 用）較 短 期 間
                                                                                               內能夠精確地將估
                                                                                               計未來現金收款或
                                                                                               付款貼現的利率。

                    recorded at the proceeds received, net of direct                           工具以扣減直接發
                    issue costs.                                                               行成本後所收取的
                                                                                               所得款項記賬。


           (vi)     Derecognition                                                       (vi)   終止確認

                    The Group derecognises a financial asset when                              當與金融資產有關
                    the contractual rights to the future cash flows                            之未來現金流合約
                    in relation to the financial asset expire or when                          權利屆滿時，或當
                    the financial asset has been transferred and the                           金融資產已轉讓，
                    transfer meets the criteria for derecognition in                           且該轉讓按照香港
                    accordance with HKFRS 9.                                                   財務報告準則第9號
                                                                                               符合終止確認標準
                                                                                               時，本集團會終止
                                                                                               確認金融資產。


                    Financial liabilities are derecognised when the                            金融負債於相關合
                    obligation specified in the relevant contract is                           約內訂明之責任解
                    discharged, cancelled or expires.                                          除、註銷或屆滿時
                                                                                               終止確認。

7.   REVENUE                                                                       7.   營業額
     Revenue from continuing operations represents amount                               來自持續性業務的營業額指年內扣
     received and receivable from sale of natural uranium, net of                       除退貨、允許折扣及相關銷售稅後

             The above listed equity investments represent ordinary               上述上市股本投資指於多倫多上市
             shares of an entity listed in Toronto. These investments are         實體的普通股。該等投資並非持作
             not held for trading, instead, they are held for long-term           買賣，相反，該等投資持作長期策
             strategic purposes. The directors of the Company have                略目的。由於本公司董事相信，於
             elected to designate these investments in equity instruments         損益確認該等投資公允值的短期波
             as FVTOCI as they believe that recognising short-term                動將不符合本集團將該等投資持作
             fluctuations in these investments’ fair value in profit or loss      長期目的並實現其長期業績潛力的
             would not be consistent with the Group’s strategy of holding         策略，故彼等已選擇將於該等權益
             these investments for long-term purposes and realising their         工具之投資指定為按公允值計入其
             performance potential in the long run.                               他全面收益。

             The Group measures the loss allowance for trade receivables          本集團計量的應收賬款虧損撥備等
             at an amount equal to lifetime ECLs. The ECLs on trade               於全期預期信貸虧損。應收賬款之
             receivables are estimated using an individual basis by               預期信貸虧損乃經參考債務人過往
             reference to past default experience and creditworthiness of         違約記錄及信譽以及對債務人現時
             the debtor and an analysis of the debtor’s current financial         財務狀況的分析後採用個別基準估
             position, adjusted for factors that are specific to the debtors,     計，並就債務人的特定因素、債務
             general economic conditions of the industry in which the             人營運所處行業的整體經濟情況以
             debtors operate and an assessment of both the current as             及對報告日期現時和預測狀況方向
             well as the forecast direction of conditions at the reporting        的評估予以調整。
             date.


             For other receivables, the Group measures the loss allowance         就其他應收款項而言，本集團計量
             equal to 12-month ECLs, unless when there is a significant           與12個月預期信貸虧損等額的虧損
             increase in credit risk since initial recognition, the Group         撥備，除非信貸風險自首次確認以
             recognises lifetime ECLs. The assessment of whether lifetime         來顯著增加，則本集團會確認全期
             ECLs should be recognised is based on significant increase           預期信貸虧損。評估是否應確認全
             in the likelihood or risk of a default occurring since initial       期預期信貸虧損，乃基於自首次確
             recognition.                                                         認起出現違約的可能性或風險是否
                                                                                  顯著增加。


             There has been no change in the estimation techniques or             於本報告期內所作估算技術或重大
             significant assumptions made during the current reporting            假設並無任何變動。
             period.

      39. FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                               39. 財務風險管理
             The main risks arising from the Group’s financial instruments          在本集團日常業務過程中，本集團
             in the normal course of the Group’s business are credit risk,          來自金融工具的主要風險為信貸風
             liquidity risk, interest rate risk and currency risk.                  險、流動資金風險、利率風險及貨
                                                                                    幣風險。


             (a)     Credit risk                                                    (a)   信貸風險

                     The Group’s maximum exposure to credit risk which                    因交易方未能履行責任而
                     will cause a financial loss to the Group due to failure to           令本集團蒙受財務虧損之
                     discharge an obligation by the counterparties is arising             最高信貸風險乃來自綜合
                     from the carrying amount of the respective recognised                財務狀況表所載列相關已
                     financial assets as stated in the consolidated statement             確認金融資產之賬面值。
                     of financial position.


                     In order to minimise the credit risk, the management of              為將信貸風險降至最低，
                     the Group has delegated a team responsible to ensure                 本集團管理層已委派一支
                     that follow-up action is taken to recover overdue                    團隊負責確保跟進收回逾
                     debts. In addition, the Group reviews the recoverability             期債項之情況。此外，本
                     of each individual trade debt at the end of the reporting            集團會於報告期末檢討各
                     period to ensure that adequate impairment losses are                 個別應收賬款之可收回
                     made for irrecoverable amounts.                                      性，確保就不可收回金額
                                                                                          作出足夠減值虧損。


                     For trade receivables, the Group has applied the                     就應收賬款而言，本集團
                     simplified approach in HKFRS 9 to measure the loss                   已應用香港財務報告準則
                     allowance at lifetime ECLs. The Group determines                     第9號的簡化方法以按全
                     the ECLs for customer with significant balances                      期預期信貸虧損計量虧損
                     by using an individual basis, estimated based on                     撥備。本集團對具重大結
                     historical credit loss experience, as well as the general            存客戶按個別基礎估算，
                     economic conditions of the industry in which the                     以債務人過往信貸虧損經
                     debtors operate. In this regard, the directors of the                驗及債務人經營行業的整
                     Company consider that the Group has a low historical                 體經濟狀況釐定預期信貸
                     credit loss experience and there was no significant and              虧損。就此而言，本公司
                     adverse changes in the general economic conditions                   的董事認為本集團過往信
                     of the industry in what the debtors operate, thus the                貸虧損為低，由於債務人
                     management considered the credit risk is not material.               經營行業的整體經濟狀況
                                                                                          並無重大不利變動，因此
                                                                                          管理層認為信貸風險不重
                                                                                          大。

                     The Group’s exposure to credit risk                                   本集團面臨的信貸風險

                     In order to minimise credit risk, the Group has tasked                為盡量降低信貸風險，本
                     its management to develop and maintain the Group’s                    集團已分派其管理人員設
                     credit risk grading to categories exposures according                 立並維持本集團的信貸風
                     to their degree of risk of default. The credit rating                 險評級，以根據違約風險
                     information is drawn from publicly available financial                程度將風險分類。信貸評

             (d)     Currency risk                                                 (d)   貨幣風險

                     The Group has transactional currency exposures.                     本集團面對交易貨幣風
                     Such exposures arise from operating activities by the               險。有關風險源自本集團
                     Group in currencies other than the entities’ functional             以該等實體的功能貨幣以

40. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                              40. 資本風險管理
   The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the            本集團管理其資本，以確保本集團
   Group will be able to continue as a going concern while                 旗下實體能夠持續經營業務，並透
   maximising the return to shareholders through the optimisation          過更完善平衡債務及權益結餘，以
   of the debt and equity balance. The Group’s overall strategy            為股東帶來最大回報。本集團整體
   remains unchanged from prior year.                                      策略與去年維持不變。


   The capital structure of the Group consists of net debt, which          本集團之資本結構包括淨負債（包
   includes loans from a fellow subsidiary and an intermediate             括應收一間同系附屬公司及一間中
   holding company, bank borrowings, net of cash and cash                  間控股公司貸款及銀行借款），扣
   equivalents, and equity attributable to owners of the Company,          除現金及現金等值項目以及本公司
   comprising issued share capital and reserves. The directors of          擁有人應佔權益（當中包括已發行
   the Company review the capital structure on a regular basis. As         股本及儲備）。本公司董事定期檢
   a part of this review, the directors of the Company consider            討資本結構。作為該檢討其中一
   the cost of capital and the risks associated with each class            環，本公司董事考慮資本成本及與
   of capital, and take appropriate actions to adjust the Group’s          各類資本相關的風險，並就此採取
   capital structure.                                                      適當行動調整本集團資本結構。



   Consistent with others in the industry, the Group monitors              與業內其他公司做法一致，本集團
   capital on the basis of the gearing ratio. This ratio is calculated     以資本負債比率監察資本。該比率
   as net debt divided by total capital. Net debt is calculated as         以淨負債除以資本總額計算。淨負
   total borrowings less cash and cash equivalents. Total capital is       債按借貸總額減現金及現金等值項
   calculated as equity as shown in the consolidated statement             目計算。資本總額按綜合財務狀況
   of financial position plus net debt.                                    表所列的權益加上淨負債計算。


   The gearing ratios at 31 December 2025 and 2024 were as                 於2025年及2024年12月31日的資
   follows:                                                                本負債比率如下：


                                                                                 2025        2024
                                                                               2025年       2024年
                                                                              HK$’000     HK$’000
                                                                               千港元         千港元


    Net debt                                  淨負債                            2,097,481   1,762,121
    Total equity                              權益總額                           4,563,104   3,921,706

                                                        stock code “PALAF” and on the Toronto Stock Exchange with the
                                                        stock code “PDN”.
      「Paladin公司」                             指         Paladin Energy Limited，一家註冊於澳大利亞並專注於進行天然鈾
                                                        開發的公司，以代號「PDN」於澳大利亞證券交易所上市，以代號
                                                        「PALAF」於美國OTCQX交易市場上市及以代號「PDN」於多倫多證券
                                                        交易所上市。


      “PRC” or “China”                        means     The People’s Republic of China.

“Semizbay-U”        means   Semizbay-U Limited Liability Partnership, a limited liability partnership
                            established in Kazakhstan, with the Company holding 49% of its
                            equity interest through its wholly-owned subsidiary and recognised as
                            a joint venture of the Company.
「謝公司」               指       謝米茲拜伊鈾有限責任合夥企業 ✽ (Semizbay-U Limited Liability
                            Partnership)，為於哈薩克斯坦成立之有限責任合夥企業，為本公司
                            合營企業，本公司通過全資附屬公司持有其49%權益。


“SFO”               means   the Securities and Futures Ordinance (Chapter 571 of the Laws of
                            Hong Kong) as amended from time to time.
「證券及期貨條例」           指       不時經修訂的《證券及期貨條例》
                                          （香港法例第571章）。

                            each.
「股份」                指       本公司每股面值0.01港元之普通股。

「附屬公司」              指       具有上市規則所賦予之含義。

                            於納米比亞註冊成立之公司，為中廣核鈾業的子公司。

      Definitions
      釋義

      “treasury shares”                            means     has the meaning ascribed to it under the Listing Rules.
      「庫存股」                                        指         具有上市規則所賦予之含義。


      “tU”                                         means     tons of elemental uranium.
      「tU」                                         指         噸金屬鈾。


      “U3O8”                                       means     Triuranium octaoxide, a compound of uranium present as an olive
                                                             green to black, odorless solid. It is one of the more popular forms
                                                             of yellowcake and is transported between mills and refineries in this
                                                             form.
      「U3O8」                                       指         八氧化三鈾，一種鈾的化合物，呈橄欖綠至黑色，無味的固體，為
                                                             比較受歡迎的黃餅形式之一，並以該形式在工廠及煉油廠之間運
                                                             輸。


      “UK”                                         means     the United Kingdom of Great Britain and Northern Ireland.
      「英國」                                         指         大不列顛及北愛爾蘭聯合王國。


      “US”                                         means     the United States of America.
      「美國」                                         指         美利堅合眾國。


      “USD” or “US$”                               means     United States dollars, the lawful currency of the US.
      「美元」或「US$」                                   指         美元，美國之法定貨幣。


      “UxC”                                        means     UxC, LLC, one of the leading providers of uranium prices and an
                                                             independent third party.
      「UxC」                                        指         UxC, LLC，為鈾價格的領先提供者之一，並為獨立第三方。


      “Zhalpak Deposit”                            means     the uranium deposit located in Sozak district, Kazakhstan, which is
                                                             owned and operated by Ortalyk.
      「扎礦」                                         指         位於哈薩克斯坦索扎克地區的鈾礦，由奧公司擁有及管理。
